## Auth/OAuth read-only verification — 2026-11-16

- `profiles` table RLS is self/admin scoped. Authenticated table grants are paired with RLS; the sensitive-field trigger blocks users from changing role/email, and no DELETE policy exists. `create_profile_on_login`/`handle_new_user` are security-definer trigger functions with fixed/qualified references and no browser EXECUTE grant. `private.current_admin_role` and `private.is_admin_user` use empty search_path; `admin_role_assignments` has service-role-only mutation and no soft-disable column (revocation is removal of the assignment).
- The auth proxy canonicalizes the production host, requires Supabase `getUser` on protected pages and sends only safe relative paths to login. Browser Supabase client uses anon key only; server client uses service role only in server-side paths. One defense-in-depth note remains: `src/lib/supabase/server.ts` does not explicitly import `server-only`, although it imports `next/headers`/Node APIs and no client import was found.
- Google OAuth uses Supabase PKCE, canonical origin and safe internal next path; the native callback accepts only the exact production bridge origin and does not place access tokens in the custom-scheme URL. Social OAuth state is HMAC-bound to provider/admin/redirect/nonce, expires within 600 seconds and uses timing-safe comparison. Facebook page scopes/tasks and YouTube upload/read/analytics scopes are validated; YouTube delete scope is not requested. OAuth tokens are stored in Supabase Vault, with secret names only in database rows.
- `profiles`/OAuth migrations reviewed and confirmed applied in live migration history. No login, OAuth exchange, profile update or credential mutation was performed.

The list API is invalidated with `revalidatePath("/api/kids/content")` after a new item is published.

Owner/content reviewer must review those 20 stories individually before marking any age-approved; no bulk flag update or content mutation was performed.
- **Kids offline snapshot:** 45 rows, all published and active, 0 stories lacking age approval. The exporter filters to published+active and age-approved stories, so the 20 legacy stories are excluded from offline fallback. Snapshot generated 2026-11-11; during a complete backend outage the app may serve this last-validated snapshot, which can be stale if content was unpublished after generation. This is an explicit offline-availability tradeoff requiring owner acceptance.
- **SEO/RBAC repair:** `seo_manager` no longer receives broad `settings.manage`; it receives dedicated `site.metadata.manage`. ## Quran tafsir restoration finding — 2026-11-07

The canonical production project currently contains all 114 surahs and 6,236 ayahs, but `public.quran_tafsir` has zero rows. The repository retains nine original, source-attributed `التفسير الميسر` seed migrations (AlQuranCloud `ar.muyassar`) covering exactly 6,236 unique ayah pairs across all 114 surahs. Their original `WHERE EXISTS (quran_surahs)` guard meant they could be marked applied before the surah seed data existed, leaving the table empty without migration-ledger drift.

PR follow-up branch `fix/quran-tafsir-backfill-release-preflight-20261107` adds a controlled correction: pre-deploy verification accepts only the known absent state (0) or complete state (6,236), production schema migrations run first, then the original nine immutable upsert-only source chunks are replayed atomically, and post-migration verification requires exactly 6,236 non-empty source-attributed records with matching Quran ayah coverage. Other tafsir authors are not changed. No production tafsir rows have been written during this audit; the restore runs only inside the protected release workflow.

## 2026-11-16 exact-head continuation — Kids social catalog reconciliation

**State: OPEN / NOT MERGE-READY.** PR #320 remains on `audit/zikr-kids-daily-priority-20261110` against `main@c4560d9ccb222bea5814cc4db32d4c3f1a2101b3`. Exact code/test repair commit: `9f5b728063cd6ed2664d7bf8420e1ed7f45e7d51`.

- **Reproducible defect:** `syncKidsSocialPublicationToCatalog` swallowed catalog read/CAS failures and returned no outcome. The queue row could remain terminal `published`, leaving no independent retry path for `videos.metadata.socialPublication`. The YouTube catalog pointer has a separate media-reconciliation path; this repair is specific to Kids social-publication metadata.
- **Repair:** Kids catalog synchronization now returns an explicit success/failure result and avoids rewriting metadata already in sync. A bounded, opt-in worker reconciler scans only already-published Kids video queue rows with a current QA-pass marker and missing/pending sync state; it revalidates the linked generation request and current human-approval fingerprint before changing the internal catalog. Queue and catalog writes use exact `updated_at` compare-and-set revisions. It never calls Facebook/YouTube, republishes, deletes media, or alters remote posts.
- **Retry safety:** at most 100 queue rows are inventoried per run; the full bounded page set is read before queue-state writes so offset pagination cannot skip rows as the pending filter changes. Sync failures retry up to eight attempts, then move to a manual-review hold. Approval-blocked rows are held immediately. `SOCIAL_KIDS_CATALOG_RECONCILIATION_ENABLED` defaults to `false` in `.env.example`; production enablement is an explicit owner decision.
- **Regression coverage:** added `scripts/tests/social-kids-catalog-reconciliation.test.ts` to the canonical `pnpm test` chain; `scripts/verify-social-publishing.mjs` now verifies the opt-in, bounded, provider-free reconciler. Tests cover queue-result consistency, partial provider results, idempotence, query bounds, feature-flag defaults, and retry exhaustion.
- **Verification:** Git-data commit `9f5b728063cd6ed2664d7bf8420e1ed7f45e7d51` contains the code/test batch. GitHub Actions checks for the exact code/test commit have not yet been observed; a follow-up documentation commit is being used to ensure the PR synchronization checks execute. Do not interpret prior-head green checks as verification of this batch.
- **Safety:** no worker was run, no content was generated, no Gemini/provider request or social publish was made, and no production database/catalog/queue row was changed. The reconciler is code-only and remains disabled by default.
- **Unchanged release blockers:** owner/CODEOWNERS review, overlapping PR reconciliation, pending Supabase migrations and production RLS rollout, production publisher hold/reconciliation, missing iOS associated-domain App ID/GA4 values, signing-secret visibility review, physical-device acceptance, merge, deployment and production smoke checks remain open.

### Reproducible defects and repair branches

1. **Favorites UUID validation — PR #352.** The DELETE service previously checked only non-empty length before passing an ID to a UUID column, allowing PostgreSQL 22P02 to become HTTP 500. UUID validation and focused regression coverage are on exact head `10e50510b1873ea66b3f0bd8e22e75b072956509`. Quality Gate run `43916794764` completed with `QUALITY_GATE=PASSED_FULL`; CircleCI `config-guard`, `application-build-docs`, and `source-verify` passed. Draft remains unmerged pending CODEOWNERS review.
2. **Legacy Kids result retry idempotency — PR #353.** The deprecated `submit-result` endpoint generated a new random ID when the client omitted a stable key. It now accepts a body ID or `Idempotency-Key` header and otherwise relies on shared validation to return 400.
3. **Concurrent Kids result retry race — PR #353.** The production-applied RPC performed a duplicate SELECT before INSERT, while the per-user advisory lock lived only in the later insert trigger. Concurrent calls could both see no row and the loser could receive unique violation 23505/HTTP 500. The PR-only migration `20261116180000_serialize_kids_game_result_idempotency.sql` adds a two-key transaction advisory lock before the lookup. A PostgreSQL 17 integration test starts 12 concurrent calls and asserts one result row, one XP/progress increment, one non-duplicate response, and eleven duplicate responses.
   - First PR #353 batch head `4fb5775a110aa66d89715e1ae865f5a1746a8418` passed Quality Gate run `43917757703` with `QUALITY_GATE=PASSED_FULL`.
   - Current PR #353 head `88b6560e52a3624d22cc8d4ca0145b2bbb6f3d7e` includes the new migration and concurrency test; Quality Gate run `43919595999` and CircleCI `source-verify` were pending at the last check. It is not closed until those exact-head checks pass.
